Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the elements framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 813. The resulting operator achieves bound O(320/n^3).

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to elements theory, we decompose the problem into 813 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 320. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(3/n). QED.
